else\",{},\"f61250b9-ca7b-4869-9837-3d32de996c9e\",{\"_299\":300,\"_301\":-5,\"_103\":583},1791058277.76009,1791058290.84677,{\"_294\":295,\"_296\":581},{\"_109\":542,\"_111\":17,\"_112\":543,\"_114\":544,\"_116\":545,\"_118\":50,\"_119\":546,\"_121\":547,\"_123\":124,\"_125\":17,\"_126\":548,\"_128\":129,\"_130\":549,\"_132\":549,\"_133\":50,\"_49\":50,\"_134\":17,\"_135\":550,\"_137\":551},{\"_289\":290,\"_291\":580},[],[],[],{\"_139\":552,\"_141\":553,\"_143\":554,\"_145\":555,\"_147\":556,\"_149\":557,\"_151\":558,\"_153\":559,\"_155\":560,\"_157\":561,\"_159\":562,\"_161\":563,\"_163\":564,\"_165\":565},[],\"43818200-5583-4307-87cf-5be398a041b4\",\"3b027511-5e90-434c-a11c-ebffa092b4ac\",\"abc347b5-b39a-4b0e-85e3-6eb5fed61a98\",[],{\"_92\":579,\"_238\":43,\"_239\":43,\"_240\":-5},{\"_92\":578,\"_238\":43,\"_239\":43,\"_240\":-5},{\"_92\":577,\"_238\":43,\"_239\":43,\"_240\":-5},{\"_92\":576,\"_238\":43,\"_239\":43,\"_240\":-5},{\"_92\":575,\"_238\":43,\"_239\":43,\"_240\":-5},{\"_92\":574,\"_238\":43,\"_239\":43,\"_240\":-5},{\"_92\":573,\"_238\":43,\"_239\":43,\"_240\":-5},{\"_92\":572,\"_238\":43,\"_239\":43,\"_240\":-5},{\"_92\":571,\"_238\":43,\"_239\":43,\"_240\":-5},{\"_92\":570,\"_238\":43,\"_239\":43,\"_240\":-5},{\"_92\":569,\"_238\":43,\"_239\":43,\"_240\":-5},{\"_92\":568,\"_238\":43,\"_239\":43,\"_240\":-5},{\"_92\":567,\"_238\":43,\"_239\":43,\"_240\":-5},{\"_92\":566,\"_238\":43,\"_239\":43,\"_240\":-5},\"jao6m6\",\"7v5e9k\",\"u1bsb5\",\"albrtq\",\"ljdgap\",\"ckb4v7\",\"s9638k\",\"6m3wli\",\"lsjifl\",\"88aqpr\",\"ntdinc\",\"tqqx4t\",\"bjlqeb\",\"fkp5g7\",[293],[582],\"Yes. The core idea is already good, but **stock correlation alone will make it look like a finance analytics project**. Since you're positioning yourself around **Cloud + AI + infrastructure**, I'd turn it into a full **AI-powered market intelligence and dependency graph platform**.\\n\\n### 1. Event → Impact propagation engine\\n\\nInstead of only asking *“what moved with STLA?”*, detect an event and trace its potential propagation.\\n\\n```text\\nStellantis Event\\n      ↓\\nEvent Classification\\n      ↓\\nAffected Business Areas\\n      ↓\\nCompanies / Suppliers / Competitors\\n      ↓\\nExpected Exposure\\n      ↓\\nActual Stock Reaction\\n      ↓\\nHistorical Comparison\\n```\\n\\nExample:\\n\\n\u003e Stellantis announces battery-production changes\\n\\nThe system identifies:\\n\\n- Battery suppliers\\n- Lithium/cobalt exposure\\n- Battery-management companies\\n- EV competitors\\n- Manufacturing partners\\n- Logistics companies\\n\\nThen checks what actually happened in their stocks.\\n\\n---\\n\\n### 2. Build a Stellantis corporate knowledge graph\\n\\nThis could become the **most impressive component**.\\n\\nCreate relationships such as:\\n\\n```text\\n                 STELLANTIS\\n                /     |      \\\\\\n               /      |       \\\\\\n         Supplier   Partner   Competitor\\n            |          |          |\\n         Company A   Company B   Company C\\n            |\\n       Semiconductor\\n            |\\n         Company D\\n```\\n\\nEach edge can have:\\n\\n- Relationship type\\n- Start/end date\\n- Evidence\\n- Contract/partnership\\n- Geographic exposure\\n- Product exposure\\n- Confidence\\n\\nThen your AI can answer:\\n\\n\u003e “Which publicly traded companies have a direct or indirect relationship with Stellantis?”\\n\\n---\\n\\n### 3. Supply-chain shock simulator\\n\\nThis would take the project beyond historical analysis.\\n\\nAllow the user to select:\\n\\n\u003e **“Stellantis factory shutdown”**\\n\\nand simulate:\\n\\n```text\\nFactory shutdown\\n       ↓\\nProduction ↓\\n       ↓\\nComponent demand ↓\\n       ↓\\nSupplier revenue exposure\\n       ↓\\nPotential market impact\\n```\\n\\nDon't present the result as a stock-price prediction. Instead show **exposure scenarios**.\\n\\nFor example:\\n\\n| Company | Exposure | Relationship | Historical sensitivity |\\n|---|---:|---|---:|\\n| Company A | High | Direct supplier | High |\\n| Company B | Medium | Technology partner | Medium |\\n| Company C | Low | Industry competitor | Low |\\n\\n---\\n\\n### 4. Competitor contagion analysis\\n\\nCompare STLA with:\\n\\n- Toyota\\n- Volkswagen\\n- Ford\\n- GM\\n- BMW\\n- Mercedes-Benz\\n- Renault\\n- Hyundai/Kia\\n- Tesla\\n\\nThen determine whether an event is:\\n\\n**STLA-specific**\\n\\nor\\n\\n**automotive-sector-wide**\\n\\nThis distinction is extremely important.\\n\\nExample:\\n\\n```text\\nSTLA        -7.2%\\nFord        -0.4%\\nGM          -0.8%\\nToyota      +0.1%\\nVW          -6.9%\\n```\\n\\nThe system shouldn't simply say *“the automotive sector fell.”*\\n\\nIt should investigate whether the reaction appears concentrated around particular companies/exposures.\\n\\n---\\n\\n### 5. News → market reaction pipeline\\n\\nThis is where the AI component becomes much more meaningful.\\n\\nAutomatically ingest news and classify:\\n\\n- Earnings\\n- Production\\n- Layoffs\\n- Recalls\\n- CEO changes\\n- Partnerships\\n- M\u0026A\\n- EV strategy\\n- Tariffs\\n- Regulations\\n- Factory closures\\n- Supply-chain disruptions\\n- Product launches\\n\\nThen connect:\\n\\n```text\\nNews article\\n     ↓\\nNLP classification\\n     ↓\\nSTLA relevance score\\n     ↓\\nEvent severity\\n     ↓\\nAffected companies\\n     ↓\\nMarket reaction\\n```\\n\\n---\\n\\n### 6. Sentiment vs actual market movement\\n\\nDon't just use generic sentiment.\\n\\nCompare:\\n\\n**News sentiment**\\n\\nvs.\\n\\n**Actual abnormal return**\\n\\nExample:\\n\\n```text\\nPositive article\\n      ↓\\nSentiment = +0.82\\n      ↓\\nSTLA = -4.3%\\n```\\n\\nYour system can flag:\\n\\n\u003e **Sentiment/market divergence**\\n\\nThose are particularly interesting events to investigate.\\n\\n---\\n\\n### 7. Lead-lag detection\\n\\nThis is another strong quantitative feature.\\n\\nInstead of only:\\n\\n\u003e “Does Company A move when STLA moves?”\\n\\nask:\\n\\n\u003e “Does Company A historically move before or after STLA?”\\n\\nTest:\\n\\n```text\\nCompany A(t-3) → STLA(t)\\n\\nCompany A(t-2) → STLA(t)\\n\\nCompany A(t-1) → STLA(t)\\n\\nSTLA(t) → Company A(t+1)\\n\\nSTLA(t) → Company A(t+3)\\n```\\n\\nThis could reveal interesting relationships without claiming predictive causation.\\n\\n---\\n\\n### 8. Regime detection\\n\\nRelationships can change.\\n\\nFor example:\\n\\n```text\\n2020–2022\\nSTLA ↔ Supplier A\\nCorrelation = 0.25\\n\\n2023–2024\\nCorrelation = 0.48\\n\\n2025–2026\\nCorrelation = 0.72\\n```\\n\\nYour system should detect:\\n\\n\u003e **Relationship strengthening / weakening**\\n\\nThis is much more interesting than calculating one correlation over the entire history.\\n\\n---\\n\\n### 9. Geographic exposure\\n\\nMap companies by geography:\\n\\n```text\\nEurope\\n ├── France\\n ├── Italy\\n ├── Germany\\n └── Poland\\n\\nNorth America\\n ├── USA\\n ├── Canada\\n └── Mexico\\n\\nAsia\\n ├── China\\n ├── Japan\\n └── Korea\\n```\\n\\nThen combine geographic exposure with events.\\n\\nFor example:\\n\\n\u003e “Which publicly traded companies could be exposed to a production disruption in Stellantis' European operations?”\\n\\n---\\n\\n### 10. Commodity exposure\\n\\nAdd:\\n\\n- Lithium\\n- Nickel\\n- Cobalt\\n- Copper\\n- Aluminum\\n- Steel\\n- Oil\\n\\nThen create:\\n\\n```text\\nCommodity shock\\n      ↓\\nAutomotive cost structure\\n      ↓\\nSTLA\\n      ↓\\nSuppliers / competitors\\n```\\n\\nThis makes the project much more like **ecosystem intelligence** rather than just stock analytics.\\n\\n---\\n\\n### 11. Earnings-call intelligence\\n\\nThis is an excellent AI feature.\\n\\nProcess earnings calls from STLA and related companies.\\n\\nExtract:\\n\\n- Supplier mentions\\n- Competitor mentions\\n- Production guidance\\n- EV strategy\\n- Battery strategy\\n- Cost pressures\\n- Geographic exposure\\n- Management sentiment\\n- Forward-looking statements\\n\\nThen build:\\n\\n\u003e **“What changed since the previous earnings call?”**\\n\\n---\\n\\n### 12. Explainable AI\\n\\nEvery AI conclusion should have an evidence chain.\\n\\nInstead of:\\n\\n\u003e “Company X will probably be affected.”\\n\\nShow:\\n\\n```text\\nWHY?\\n\\n1. Company X is a documented supplier.\\n2. Supplier relationship began in 2024.\\n3. 6 historical STLA events showed correlated movement.\\n4. Median event-window reaction = X%.\\n5. Latest event concerns the supplied component.\\n6. Supporting filing/news: [source]\\n```\\n\\nThis makes the AI considerably more credible.\\n\\n---\\n\\n### 13. Confidence scoring\\n\\nGive every relationship a **confidence level**, not an investment rating.\\n\\nFor example:\\n\\n```text\\nRelationship confidence\\n████████████████░░ 82%\\n\\nEvidence:\\n✓ Direct supplier relationship\\n✓ Multiple historical events\\n✓ Statistically significant correlation\\n✓ Recent filing confirms relationship\\n✓ Similar historical event\\n```\\n\\nThis is different from saying the stock is a “buy” or “sell.”\\n\\n---\\n\\n### 14. Ask-your-data AI agent\\n\\nThis could be your frontend's killer feature.\\n\\nUser:\\n\\n\u003e **“What happened to companies connected to Stellantis during the last major production disruption?”**\\n\\nAgent:\\n\\n```text\\nIntent detection\\n       ↓\\nQuery financial DB\\n       ↓\\nQuery relationship graph\\n       ↓\\nRetrieve news/filings\\n       ↓\\nRun statistical analysis\\n       ↓\\nLLM synthesis\\n       ↓\\nAnswer + evidence\\n```\\n\\nOther questions:\\n\\n\u003e “Show me STLA's strongest supplier relationships.”\\n\\n\u003e “Which companies historically reacted most strongly to STLA events?”\\n\\n\u003e “Was the 2025 decline STLA-specific or sector-wide?”\\n\\n\u003e “Which suppliers have become more exposed to STLA over time?”\\n\\n\u003e “Show me companies connected to Stellantis through batteries.”\\n\\n---\\n\\n## 15. Make the architecture match your Cloud Developer profile\\n\\nI'd build it roughly like this:\\n\\n```text\\n                 DATA SOURCES\\n                     │\\n       ┌─────────────┼─────────────┐\\n       ↓             ↓             ↓\\n   Market Data     News        SEC/Filings\\n       │             │             │\\n       └─────────────┼─────────────┘\\n                     ↓\\n              Data Ingestion\\n                     ↓\\n              ┌──────────────┐\\n              │ Object Store │\\n              │ S3 / Azure   │\\n              │ Blob         │\\n              └──────┬───────┘\\n                     ↓\\n             Data Processing\\n                     ↓\\n              ┌──────────────┐\\n              │ Spark / dbt  │\\n              └──────┬───────┘\\n                     ↓\\n             ┌───────────────┐\\n             │ Data Warehouse│\\n             └───────┬───────┘\\n                     ↓\\n       ┌─────────────┼─────────────┐\\n       ↓             ↓             ↓\\n  Event Engine   Correlation    Knowledge\\n                 Engine         Graph\\n       │             │             │\\n       └─────────────┼─────────────┘\\n                     ↓\\n                  AI Layer\\n                     ↓\\n              RAG + LLM Agent\\n                     ↓\\n             FastAPI / APIs\\n                     ↓\\n             React Dashboard\\n```\\n\\n### And add observability\\n\\nSince you're working toward **cloud + infrastructure + AI**, add:\\n\\n- Docker\\n- Kubernetes\\n- Terraform\\n- CI/CD\\n- OpenTelemetry\\n- Prometheus/Grafana\\n- API Gateway\\n- Authentication\\n- Logging\\n- Data-quality checks\\n- Pipeline monitoring\\n\\nThen the project demonstrates much more than finance.\\n\\n---\\n\\n## The final project could have 5 major modules\\n\\n**① Market Intelligence**\\n\\n\u003e What happened to STLA?\\n\\n**② Impact Intelligence**\\n\\n\u003e What other companies moved?\\n\\n**③ Relationship Intelligence**\\n\\n\u003e Why are those companies connected?\\n\\n**④ AI Intelligence**\\n\\n\u003e What does the evidence suggest caused the relationship?\\n\\n**⑤ Infrastructure**\\n\\n\u003e How do we ingest, process, store, analyze and serve all of this reliably?\\n\\nThat gives you a genuinely substantial **Cloud + Data Engineering + AI project**, rather than just a stock-correlation dashboard.\",{},{\"_299\":313,\"_301\":-5,\"_103\":591},1791058277.438,1791058292.170466,{\"_294\":295,\"_296\":589},{\"_126\":548,\"_309\":-5,\"_130\":549,\"_132\":549,\"_134\":43},[590],\"what else can i add to this
